Use when a test fails only in CI, local runs pass, and the signature smells like memory pressure (exit 137, OOM kills). Confirm the kill in CI logs, then raise the runner's memory or slim the test's footprint. Not for failures with no memory signal, and not for accepted heavy-by-design tests. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-kept-re-running-a-failing-tes-3586d5030cf5660f))
- **my-agent-labeled-intermittent-db-deadl-c2561523fde40fe5**: Fixes agents that dismiss intermittent database deadlocks as flakes instead of filing the transaction-ordering bug. Use when parallel tests deadlock sporadically and the deadlock graph shows the same tables locked in different order by two transactions. Capture the deadlock report, enforce one consistent lock order in code, and keep retries as a backstop only. Not for deadlocks from an overloaded database, and not for single-threaded runs with no concurrency. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-labeled-intermittent-db-deadl-c2561523fde40fe5))
- **my-agent-name**: What this agent does, for whom, in one sentence. Use when: the situation that calls for it. ([Community-Access/glow](https://github.com/Community-Access/glow/tree/HEAD/docs/ahg-2026/kit/my-agent) · ★4)
- **my-agent-quarantined-a-test-that-faile-ab0794e997acaeba**: Teaches an agent how to tell a true one-in-200 race condition from random noise before quarantining, and how to recover when a real bug got quarantined. Use when a rarely failing test was auto-quarantined and a later investigation found a real concurrency bug. Not for bulk-quarantine cleanup, deterministic failures mislabeled as flaky, or tests that fail on every run. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-quarantined-a-test-that-faile-ab0794e997acaeba))
- **my-agent-quarantined-a-whole-spec-file-b342b59b4df61e2b**: Recovers signal buried when an agent quarantined an entire spec file even though a single retry would have passed. Use it when whole-file quarantine hid a real failure that only shows up in full-suite context. Not for files with several independently failing tests, and not for ordering bugs inside the file. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-quarantined-a-whole-spec-file-b342b59b4df61e2b))
- **my-agent-quarantined-every-test-in-the-25e6ed881a8b95fd**: Corrects the mistake of quarantining every test in a file when one broken test is polluting the rest. Use it when a whole file got skipped after a single failure and the agent never isolated the actual culprit. Not for files where multiple tests are independently flaky, and not for ordering dependencies between files. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-quarantined-every-test-in-the-25e6ed881a8b95fd))
- **my-agent-ran-the-failing-test-alone-an-a6a6a90fe671f9f2**: Explains why running a failing test alone says not reproducible when CI shuffles order with pytest-randomly. Use it when the agent ran the test solo and gave up, but CI fails it under a shuffled seed. Not for order-independent flakes, and not for suites that do not use random ordering. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-ran-the-failing-test-alone-an-a6a6a90fe671f9f2))
- **my-agent-replaced-a-racy-assertion-wit-4b260aef5d3f9403**: A vacuous-wait repair guide: a waitForSelector that targets the wrong element passes as soon as that element exists, asserting nothing about the behavior under test. Use when a converted wait-based test passes but no longer verifies what the original assertion checked. Not for waits that target the correct element or for genuinely passing tests. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-replaced-a-racy-assertion-wit-4b260aef5d3f9403))
- **my-agent-s-fix-made-the-assertion-wait-26033d1d7ba0492e**: A slowdown-first repair guide: when an endpoint gets dramatically slower, lengthening the test's wait just moves the failure from CI to production. Use when an agent answered a latency regression by relaxing the test instead of profiling the endpoint. Not for endpoints whose slowness is environmental or explicitly accepted. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-s-fix-made-the-assertion-wait-26033d1d7ba0492e))
- **my-agent-s-fix-was-deleting-the-test-t-8afde5d674c1d44a**: A rollback-and-repair playbook for when an agent deleted the test that was leaking state instead of fixing the leak: restore the test, find the leak at its source, and keep the restored test as the regression guard. Use when a deletion made the suite green while the underlying state pollution survives. Not for tests deleted because they were genuinely obsolete or duplicated. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-s-fix-was-deleting-the-test-t-8afde5d674c1d44a))
- **my-agent-said-can-t-reproduce-for-a-te-b6db95c05f5746f1**: Helps an agent investigate CI-only test failures caused by a nearly full runner disk. Use it when a test fails in CI with disk-space, ENOSPC, or write errors but passes locally, and the agent keeps saying it cannot reproduce the failure. Not for genuine logic bugs, and not for failures that also happen on machines with plenty of free disk. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/my-agent-said-can-t-reproduce-for-a-te-b6db95c05f5746f1))

Do NOT use for routine edits, single obvious fixes, q… ([Ynakatsuka/dotfiles](https://github.com/Ynakatsuka/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/home/dot_claude/skills/my-better) · ★4)
- **my-blog**: 我的中文博客生成 skill — 两阶段工作流（想法 → 草稿 → 终稿补丁）+ 风格 DNA 兜底 + 显式串行调 deai 做 AI 痕迹清除 + Soul-Loss Guard 反"中性说明书"。读完 03-final.md 让别人 100% 相信是我自己写的。用户触发 /my-blog 或在 ~/wpx/my/my-blog 项目里手动调用。 ([Fancu1/dotfile](https://github.com/Fancu1/dotfile/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/my-blog))
- **my-blueprint**: The author's personal longevity blueprint — diet, workout schedule, morning routine, and integrated wellness system to use as a template for building your own. ([coachmanjeet/agilefitness](https://github.com/coachmanjeet/agilefitness/tree/HEAD/skills/my-blueprint) · ★2)
- **my-bookings**: Find a Getflights booking — by booking reference, travel date, destination or group size — and see where it stands. Use whenever someone asks about their booking, when or where they travel, what it cost, or whether their tickets have been issued. ([getflights/claude](https://github.com/getflights/claude/tree/HEAD/skills/my-bookings) · 2 copies)
- **my-brain**: The user's core engineering principles — everything as code, decoupled, structured (types first), scalable, measured. Use when designing, writing, reviewing, or scaffolding any code, config, or infrastructure, or when choosing between approaches, so the work matches how this user builds things. ([knvpk/Agentic](https://github.com/knvpk/Agentic/tree/HEAD/skills/my-brain))
- **my-brain-capture**: Automatically invoked after work that produces durable knowledge worth preserving beyond current session. Triggers on significant decisions, non-obvious bug fixes, conventions, or explicit user intent like "remember this", "save this", "for future", "going forward". Does not trigger on routine acknowledgments or trivial edits. ([rafaelcmm/my-brain](https://github.com/rafaelcmm/my-brain/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/my-brain-capture))
- **my-brain-context**: Automatically invoked at the start of any non-trivial response to silently enrich working context with relevant long-term memory from my-brain. Triggers when user asks questions that may depend on prior decisions, prior sessions, code conventions, architectural choices, or context outside current window. Signal phrases include references like "we decided", "last time", "earlier", "continue", or requests to extend existing systems. ([rafaelcmm/my-brain](https://github.com/rafaelcmm/my-brain/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/my-brain-context))
